HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_14b5c5b3cdad016e91b9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:7:4\",\"root_000569\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Karşılıklı hoşnutluk boyutu, ödülü sahip olunan bir şeyden kişi ile yaşamın birbirini kabul ettiği bir ilişkiye dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001067/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2a970473fbb9de586eae","text":"1. **The life-condition itself is the satisfied subject.** `ع ي ش B001` includes life and the quality of a lived condition, while `ر ض و B001` includes active acceptance opposed to displeasure. Grammatically, the feminine active adjective `رَّاضِيَةٍ` agrees with `عِيشَةٍ`; the condition, not an explicitly named inhabitant, bears satisfaction. The branch role is personification of a life that accepts or fits the person delivered to it by `فَهُوَ`. I infer that grammatical agency signals suitability rather than literal emotion. The ayah reads as congruence between person and condition, not merely private pleasure within it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5e9ae2bb7eea7d29d6fe","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:7:4\",\"root_000569\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yaşamın güvence veren ve seven bir özne gibi davranması, ağır bulunan kişiyi koruyan bir teminat izlenimi doğurur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7db947218b00ea5651b0","text":"2. **Balance becomes reciprocal fit.** `ر ض و B003` supplies satisfaction by mutual consent. The heavy balances of 101:6 and the locative relation `فِى عِيشَةٍ` place evaluated person and resulting life in relation; the active adjective lets satisfaction point back from the condition toward its inhabitant. The branch's role is reciprocity: the person receives the life, and the life is presented as accepting the person. This mutuality is inferred because the fixed form is not morphologically reciprocal. It adds a relational outcome to the judicial mechanism rather than reducing reward to possession.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B001","root_001067/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_aa82c7fff8f7765d4057","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:7:3\",\"root_001067\",[\"B001\"]],[\"101:7:4\",\"root_000569\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hoşnutluk dilbilgisel olarak yaşamın kendisine yüklenerek, kişi ile içine yerleştiği durum arasında uygunluk kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B001","root_000569/B003","root_000569/B004","root_000569/B006","root_001056/B001","root_001067/B001","root_001067/B002","root_001143/B002","root_001332/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b555c095a143d5393d83","text":"1. Life as condition matched to weight. Evidence: ع ي ش B001, life and lived condition; ر ض و B001, acceptance opposed to displeasure; و ز ن B002/B007 in 101:6, just balance and valued standing. Structural cue: فَهُوَ marks direct consequence after ثقلت موازينه. Mechanism: adjudicated worth produces an inhabited condition that itself is accepting/accepted. Abductive move: I infer that the life is not merely pleasant but congruent with the scale's judgment. Reading change: عيشة راضية reads as an environment aligned with justice. 2. Livelihood after stripped provisions. Evidence: ع ي ش B002, means of living/livelihood; ع ه ن B001 in 101:5, ready accessible property/food/drink; ف ر ش B002, prepared resting place. Structural cue: after worldly supports are scattered/fluffed, this ayah supplies a new \"in\" space. Mechanism: the rewarded person receives not only survival but a complete provision-place. Abductive move: I supply replacement: collapsed earthly provisioning is answered by a different livelihood. Reading change: عيشة is read materially as sustaining habitation, not only abstract life. 3. Mutual consent/restored relation. Evidence: ر ض و B003, reciprocal satisfaction; ر ض و B004, making another satisfied; ر ح م B002 at 101:0, kinship bond. Structural cue: the singular \"he\" enters a feminized عيشة راضية after the communal chaos of الناس. Mechanism: isolated judgment is reabsorbed into a relation of acceptance and care. Abductive move: I infer social/affective repair from رضى branches and the opening kinship field. Reading change: the ayah reads as reconciled belonging rather than solitary reward. 4. Obedient/guaranteeing life. Evidence: ر ض و B006, raḍiyy as obedient, loving, or guaranteeing; ك و ن B003, suretyship/responsibility; ث ق ل B005, precious protected worth. Structural cue: the life follows a successful scale result. Mechanism: the new life acts almost as guarantor for the one whose weight was accepted. Abductive move: I supply a juridical protection relation from \"guaranteeing\" to the rewarded state. Reading change: راضية can be heard as a life that receives, loves, and secures its inhabitant.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c20cb6621a10b893231c","text":"101:9 retrospectively made the relational language of عيشة راضية stronger: the bad outcome also has a feminine receiving-place, but as mother/abyss. 101:11 clarified that the good \"in a life\" contrasts with being enclosed in a heated zone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001067/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d332d0d623cb208ee7c5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:7:3\",\"root_001067\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yaşamın geçim ve yaşama ortamı boyutu, bu sonucu geçici bir duygu değil kişiyi sürdüren kapsayıcı bir düzen olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003","root_000569/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d96b06c597282b6ac07a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:7:4\",\"root_000569\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sonraki ana-uçurum karşıtlığı, burada yargılanan kişinin yeniden bakım ve aidiyet ilişkisine alınmasını düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001067/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_dd688ea28194c3bb3677","text":"3. **Life as a sustaining habitat.** `ع ي ش B002` covers livelihood, provisions, and the means or place by which one lives. The preposition `فِى` frames `عِيشَةٍ` as an encompassing condition. Later, 101:9-11 also gives the opposed person an encompassing destination, first as `أُمُّهُ هَاوِيَةٌ` and then as fire. This spatial opposition assigns the livelihood branch the role of a sustaining environment. I infer that the two outcomes are structurally comparable habitats; the text does not repeat `فِى` in the negative clause. The ayah thus describes entry into a life-supporting order, not a passing feeling.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003","root_001067/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fca6b02d36c7d0deb13c","text":"The negative sequence in 101:8-11 retrospectively changed the force of `فِى`. The opposed person is re-centered in a mother/abyss and that referent is identified as fire, so `ع ي ش B002` becomes an inhabitable, sustaining environment by contrast. This also sharpened the exploratory `ر ض و B003` reading: the positive condition fits its inhabitant, whereas the negative enclosure receives without mutual satisfaction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
